| obs:wide-sparse-unary-quotient-image | Homogeneous affine tuples supported on O(N) cells leave a 1-o(1) fraction of the full degree-k functional-PHP quotient for k=o(sqrt N), even in a polynomial family with wide intermediate rank robust on every residual board of size at least N/2. | working_proof | [Unary quotient images and support-adapted endpoint](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#wide-sparse-unary-quotient-image) |
| lem:sparse-cell-matching-separation | Matching monomials supported in a cell graph of maximum row degree Delta remain linearly independent modulo old functional-PHP PC through B when N>=2B-1 and N>=Delta+2k, for targets through k<=B. | working_proof | [Unary quotient images and support-adapted endpoint](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#sparse-cell-matching-separation) |
| thm:support-sensitive-unary-affine-endpoint | A complete unary-affine ENS family admits a shared-support dimension endpoint: sum_b b_k(s-r_b)<(1-epsilon_k)choose(s,k), with the stated support and old-degree room, excludes NS and PC refutations. | working_proof | [Unary quotient images and support-adapted endpoint](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#support-sensitive-unary-affine-endpoint) |
| imp:MN-functional-graph-degree | Miksa-Nordstrom Theorem 4.9 gives graph functional-PHP PC degree greater than delta*s/(2d) for an (s,delta)-boundary expander of maximum left degree d; the bound transfers to linear rows at ceilings at least d. | established | [Graph-synchronized unary-affine weights](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#MN-functional-graph-degree) |
| lem:random-graph-unary-separated-space | A sixteen-neighbor graph with one distinguished edge per row admits a homogeneous degree-k space in 15(N+1) coordinates of dimension at least one half exp(-5k^2/N) choose(15(N+1),k), separated from old graph PC through N/10^6 for k=O(sqrt(N log N)). | working_proof | [Graph-synchronized unary-affine weights](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#random-graph-unary-separated-space) |
| thm:general-binary-unary-affine-source-exclusion | Over F2, ordinary functional or weak unary PHP with any polynomial-inventory complete one-level old-affine ENS family has no polylogarithmic-degree NS or PC refutation for sufficiently large N. | working_proof | [Graph-synchronized unary-affine weights](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#general-binary-unary-affine-source-exclusion) |
| thm:all-prime-unary-affine-source-exclusion | For every fixed prime p, unary weak or functional PHP plus any polynomial-inventory complete one-level old-affine ENS family excludes polylogarithmic-degree ordinary NS and PC refutations. | working_proof | [All-prime graph endpoint and ordinary-PHP parity audit](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#all-prime-unary-affine-source-exclusion) |
| thm:ordinary-unary-PHP-Res-parity-superpolynomial | For every real K>0, sufficiently large ordinary unary PHP and its functional variant require more than N^K nodes in unrestricted DAG-like Res(parity), under both recorded rule conventions. | working_proof | [All-prime graph endpoint and ordinary-PHP parity audit](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#ordinary-unary-PHP-Res-parity-superpolynomial) |
| obs:graph-affine-first-retained-gap | Sparse graph restriction and affine row elimination do not preserve affineness of induced zero-tests; multiplying two current square-root degree factors would exceed the old linear barrier. | working_proof | [All-prime graph endpoint and ordinary-PHP parity audit](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#graph-affine-first-retained-gap) |
| thm:power-budget-unary-affine-source-exclusion | For fixed p and 2 alpha+beta<1, unary PHP plus any complete old-affine ENS family excludes D<=N^alpha polylog N when log(M+1)<=N^beta polylog N, at arbitrary positive accuracies. | working_proof | [Graph power budget and retained low case](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#power-budget-unary-affine-source-exclusion) |
| cor:unary-private-low-polar-endpoint | A complete two-level unary PHP source with private old-affine bottoms and affine parents in their products excludes polylog-degree refutations at collective parent polar rank <=2N^eta, eta<1/3, with arbitrary positive accuracies. | working_proof | [Graph power budget and retained low case](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#unary-private-low-polar-endpoint) |
| cor:ordinary-unary-PHP-parity-near-exponential | For every fixed 0<epsilon<1, sufficiently large unary PHP and its functional variant require more than exp(N^(1-epsilon)) nodes in unrestricted DAG-like Res(parity), under both recorded rule conventions. | working_proof | [Graph power budget and retained low case](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#ordinary-unary-PHP-parity-near-exponential) |
| obs:graph-absolute-head-budget | The current absolute leading-head image bound cannot beat the guaranteed graph-weight dimension for any nonlinear width w>=2, even for one block. | working_proof | [Graph power budget and retained low case](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#graph-absolute-head-budget) |
| lem:finite-field-punctured-first-jet-degree | Over F_q, a polynomial double-vanishing at all nonzero grid points has degree at least n(q-1)+q-1 if nonzero at the origin, and at least n(q-1)+q if the origin is a simple zero; every prime power q and n>=1. | working_proof | [Complete lemma proof](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/branches/odd-field-double-covering/#finite-field-punctured-first-jet-degree) |
| thm:odd-field-double-cover-exact | For every prime power q>=2 and every n>=1, f_q(n,2,1)=(q-1)(n+1): nonzero vectors covered at least twice, origin at most once, with hyperplane repetitions allowed. | working_proof | [Exact covering theorem and construction](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/branches/odd-field-double-covering/#odd-field-double-cover-exact) |
| lem:robust-graph-compatible-pair-space | A fixed boundary expander with controlled right degree has at least one half exp(-2A0 k^2/s) choose(s,k) compatible-pair monomials whose deletions preserve half its expansion, under the stated finite conditions. | working_proof | [Fixed-graph source endpoint and relative old-target window](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#robust-graph-compatible-pair-space) |
| thm:fixed-expander-affine-source-endpoint | Every graph in the stated robust class supports an all-prime affine ENS endpoint; uniformly it handles 2 alpha+beta<1 and delta(1-beta)>8 at polynomial right degree in log N. | working_proof | [Fixed-graph source endpoint and relative old-target window](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#fixed-expander-affine-source-endpoint) |
| thm:affine-layer-graph-old-target-window | On the specified strong graph family, a large old admissible-monomial space remains separated through an arbitrary complete affine ENS layer when k<=B, k<=N^eta polylog N, B<=N^theta polylog N and 0<=eta<=theta with 2 theta+eta<1. | working_proof | [Fixed-graph source endpoint and relative old-target window](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#affine-layer-graph-old-target-window) |
| obs:product-difference-parent-kernel-obstruction | Genuine parent differences P_j-P_0 can have collective polar rank at least 4a^2 while every Boolean-only input-ideal reduction is injective on old-only multilinear subspaces. | working_proof | [Parent-kernel scope review and common reference clamp](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#product-difference-parent-kernel-obstruction) |
| thm:constant-affine-reference-source-endpoint | A complete acyclic source admits a graph common-weight endpoint when all internal products have a consistent constant reference profile with affine zero/unit/high-rank tuples, while terminal references may have arbitrary affine rank. | working_proof | [Parent-kernel scope review and common reference clamp](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#constant-affine-reference-source-endpoint) |
| thm:two-level-affine-core-semantic-map | A complete two-level affine-bottom source has a direct semantic coefficient map costing the maximum of bottom packing and one affine-core cost plus a nonlinear prefix cost. | working_proof | [Additive two-level semantic map](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#two-level-affine-core-semantic-map) |
| cor:rank-product-two-level-unary-exclusion | Two-level unary-affine-bottom sources with bottom rank N^rho and parent nonlinear quotient rank N^sigma are excluded when rho+sigma<1, up to fixed polylogarithmic factors. | working_proof | [Additive two-level semantic map](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#rank-product-two-level-unary-exclusion) |
| thm:costed-partition-direct-semantic-map | Per-block old partitions on a costed nonempty domain interpolate common affine reference normalizers with additive coefficient degree T_0+t and complete source cost (T_0+t)D+k+lambda. | working_proof | [Direct partitions on a costed PHP domain](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#costed-partition-direct-semantic-map) |
| cor:incompatible-matching-parent-source-exclusion | Two-level graph PHP sources whose parents read mutually incompatible matching indicators are excluded at polynomial inventory and polylogarithmic degree when matching size is N^rho polylog N for fixed rho<1. | working_proof | [Direct partitions on a costed PHP domain](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#incompatible-matching-parent-source-exclusion) |
| obs:weighted-matching-partition-degree | On the partial-matching domain, affine partitions of a disjoint matching-product tuple under a nonzero degree-k weight require selector degree t with t+k >= max(0,a-3k)(r-1). | working_proof | [Partition/kernel route review and companion maps](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#weighted-matching-partition-degree) |
| thm:complement-exposed-relative-parent-endpoint | Parents exposing whole-product complements admit a retained companion-based map when the summed old-affine restriction images are smaller than the separated old-target space. | working_proof | [Partition/kernel route review and companion maps](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#complement-exposed-relative-parent-endpoint) |
| thm:ancestral-affine-union-source-map | An acyclic complete source whose every input span equals the span of old affine primitives and predecessor P_c-1 values has a common old coefficient map costing T_0+1, independent of dependency depth. | working_proof | [Partition/kernel route review and companion maps](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#ancestral-affine-union-source-map) |
| audit:ancestral-union-compiler-coverage | The compiled binary source does not automatically have ancestral affine-union or complement-exposed input spans: negated ORs give positive products and MOD frames permit inseparable selector mixtures. | working_proof | [Compiler polarity and constant-span audit](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#ancestral-union-compiler-coverage) |
| lem:retained-PC-moment-pairing | Every nonrefutable retained Boolean PC space through B admits a normalized finite-extension annihilator with nonsingular pairings on P_<=t/(C_B intersect P_<=t), simultaneously at any finitely many cutoffs with 2t<=B. | working_proof | [Retained PC pairing and coupled coefficient densities](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#retained-PC-moment-pairing) |
| criterion:PC-saturated-coefficient-density-system | Independent density classes for every fresh Boolean coefficient support give exact coupled coordinates for degree-D source functionals whose retained components annihilate C_B at their cutoffs, when B>=2D. | working_proof | [Retained PC pairing and coupled coefficient densities](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#PC-saturated-coefficient-density-system) |
| thm:sublinear-feature-two-level-endpoint | Two-level unary sources with per-parent active old feature dimension N^eta polylog N exclude degree N^alpha polylog N proofs whenever 2alpha+eta<1; the low-polar consequence now allows every fixed eta<1 at polylogarithmic degree. | working_proof | [Feature budget and local-density review](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#sublinear-feature-two-level-endpoint) |
| lem:prescribed-retained-marginal-extension | Every selected fresh subfamily with 2D+sum delta_a<=B has a degree-D PC-saturated functional preserving any prescribed normalized retained C_B annihilator. | working_proof | [Feature budget and local-density review](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#prescribed-retained-marginal-extension) |
| obs:common-root-local-density-obstruction | A polynomially growing local range of saturated density solutions with the same old marginal and nonsingular pairing can coexist with an infeasible full density system. | working_proof | [Feature budget and local-density review](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#common-root-local-density-obstruction) |
| cor:affine-flat-conditioning-gap | Affine conditioning of the full functional PHP base either has a degree-(p-1)r NS refutation or retains PC hardness through D when D+(p-1)r fits the old stable range. | working_proof | [Feature budget and local-density review](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#affine-flat-conditioning-gap) |
| thm:near-balanced-unary-affine-endpoint | The all-prime unary-affine power endpoint holds for N+1<=m<=3N/2 pigeons, with the same 2alpha+beta<1 range in holes N. | working_proof | [Full old-target preservation and retained conditioning](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#near-balanced-unary-affine-endpoint) |
| thm:full-unary-affine-old-preservation | A polynomial-inventory affine ENS layer on complete functional unary PHP preserves the full old degree-t matching quotient through degree D when 0<=eta<=alpha and 2alpha+eta<1. | working_proof | [Full old-target preservation and retained conditioning](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#full-unary-affine-old-preservation) |
| cor:retained-affine-conditioning-gap | Old affine constraints added to a complete unary affine ENS layer either refute through their selector degree tau or preserve PC hardness through D under the full old-window bounds at D+tau. | working_proof | [Full old-target preservation and retained conditioning](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#retained-affine-conditioning-gap) |
| lem:coherent-retained-flat-conditionals | One retained affine-layer functional can give every live flat in a finite bounded-rank family nonzero mass and supply coherent conditional components on their allowed intersections. | working_proof | [Full old-target preservation and retained conditioning](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#coherent-retained-flat-conditionals) |
| lem:rank-aware-bottom-product-comparison | A product of affine bottom values differs from the indicator of their joint input flat by an ordinary NS certificate through its original product degree plus (p-1) times the union rank. | working_proof | [Intersection-span source map and controls](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#rank-aware-bottom-product-comparison) |
| thm:intersection-span-stratum-source-map | A common affine graph weight and low-rank span-poset inversion give a complete two-level parent map under an explicit bound on the number of generated spans below a rank cutoff. | working_proof | [Intersection-span source map and controls](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#intersection-span-stratum-source-map) |
| cor:rank-growth-stratum-endpoint | The span-stratum map has a power endpoint when log reference count has power sigma, the rank cutoff has power rho>(1+sigma)/2, and proof degree has power alpha with alpha+rho<1. | working_proof | [Intersection-span source map and controls](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#rank-growth-stratum-endpoint) |
| check:flat-stratum-map-controls | Exact F2/F3/F5 point controls verify shared-bottom stratum interpolation and demonstrate failure when the rank-crossing support restriction is removed. | finite_check | [Intersection-span source map and controls](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#flat-stratum-map-controls) |
| obs:logarithmic-span-cutoff-obstruction | Logarithmic direct-sum bottom ranks defeat the finite span-stratum cutoff budget at source degree at least a fixed positive multiple of log N; literal boundary weights have degree at least a-floor(R/r). | working_proof | [Intersection-span source map and controls](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#logarithmic-span-cutoff-obstruction) |
| cor:rank-free-flat-value-choice | For a distinct proper affine flat types over full functional PHP, one can choose their zero/one values with a nonzero old multiplier of charged degree at most (p-1)a, independent of the flat ranks. | working_proof | [Intersection-span source map and controls](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#rank-free-flat-value-choice) |
| thm:pure-reader-bottom-count-lift | Complete arbitrary-depth pure polynomial readers over affine bottoms have no degree-D PC/NS refutation of unary PHP when N>=2[TD+(p-1)a]-1, with a the number of distinct proper bottom input spans and T constant in p. | working_proof | [Intersection-span source map and controls](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#pure-reader-bottom-count-lift) |
| thm:greedy-internal-affine-reference-transfer | Adaptive greedy choices clamp internal products while retaining terminal affine blocks; a degree-D complete-source refutation yields an old target f through TD+t, with f outside old I_t and t<=(p-1)a. | working_proof | [Intersection-span source map and controls](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#greedy-internal-affine-reference-transfer) |
| cor:greedy-internal-reference-power-endpoint | Polynomial-inventory sources with affine visited references are excluded when D<=N^alpha polylog N, a<=N^eta polylog N and 2 max(alpha,eta)+eta<1. | working_proof | [Intersection-span source map and controls](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#greedy-internal-reference-power-endpoint) |
| audit:internal-reference-compiler-coverage | The binary compiled grammar supplies affine visited references, but odd-prime MOD frames need not; virtual MOD names preserve nonlinear defining equations rather than giving affine ENS inputs. | working_proof | [Intersection-span source map and controls](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#internal-reference-compiler-coverage) |
| lem:row-separated-greedy-charge-obstruction | For floor(N/4) disjoint pigeon rows with rank-two decoded-bit input spans, every unmodified greedy internal-reference trace makes a successful event selection on every block. | working_proof | [Intersection-span source map and controls](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#row-separated-greedy-charge-obstruction) |
| audit:mixed-elimination-clamping-budget | An unweighted map, reverse terminal elimination and final internal clamping have ledger E=T(AD+C)+t; no uniform compiled-source bound or free interleaving follows. | working_proof | [Intersection-span source map and controls](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#mixed-elimination-clamping-budget) |
| lem:old-bit-multiplication-annihilator | Over any field, a nonzero old functional-PHP class of degree at most t has at most t ell annihilating bit-affine directions at cutoff t+1, for N=2^ell>4t and m>=t+1. | working_proof | [Intersection-span source map and controls](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#old-bit-multiplication-annihilator) |
| cor:generic-flat-greedy-charge | For binary generic rank-ell old-bit input spans, M=Theta(N) with M<=N/8 forces charge M for every unmodified greedy trace except with probability 2^(-Omega(N ell^2)). | working_proof | [Intersection-span source map and controls](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#generic-flat-greedy-charge) |
| audit:logarithmic-flat-endpoint-gap | The existing fixed quadratic/cubic endpoints and disjoint-head ledger do not supply a uniform logarithmic-degree old-input endpoint. | working_proof | [Intersection-span source map and controls](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#logarithmic-flat-endpoint-gap) |
| cor:generic-affine-flat-avoidance-hardness | For binary uniform codimension-log N affine flats at M<=N/8 inventory, their raw avoidance equations preserve old PHP PC/NS hardness through floor((N+1)/2)-M outside a 2^(-Omega(N log^2 N)) exceptional event. | working_proof | [Intersection-span source map and controls](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#generic-affine-flat-avoidance-hardness) |
| criterion:flat-avoidance-affine-layer-transfer | For binary two-level sources with bottom rank r_c<=h_c and parent inputs old affine plus selector sums, hardness of the corresponding flat-avoidance base with its terminal affine ENS family implies source hardness at the original degree. | conditional | [Intersection-span source map and controls](https://kbr.is-a.dev/math-research/#flat-avoidance-affine-layer-transfer) |
